## Approved Plan:
# Multiple Worlds & Multiple Player Profiles

## Context

Today the game has exactly one world and one implicit player. A single localStorage
key (`minecraft_save_v7`) holds the only world; the player's appearance lives in a
separate global key (`minecraft_skin_v1`). The app boots straight into that one world —
there is no title/menu, no way to keep more than one save, and no notion of distinct
players sharing a browser.

This feature adds **multiple named worlds** and **multiple player profiles**, with the
decided model: **a profile owns its worlds** (1 profile : N worlds), like user accounts
on a shared console. Selecting a profile filters the world list. A profile carries
**name + skin only**; audio settings stay global. Shipped as **one combined feature** on
one branch.

The architecture is well-suited to this: the save layer's `readSave`/`writeSave` already
take a key parameter (always called with the one constant today), the engine is fully
headless and disposable, and the mount lifecycle already tears down everything cleanly on
unmount — so worlds can be switched at runtime via a React `key` remount, with no page
reload and no engine refactor.

Branch: `feat/worlds-and-profiles` off `main`.

## Data model (localStorage)

| Key | Shape | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `minecraft_profiles_v1` | `ProfilesManifest` | profile list + `activeProfileId` |
| `minecraft_worlds_v1` | `WorldsManifest` | world index across all profiles |
| `minecraft_world_save_<worldId>` | `SaveData` (v5, **unchanged**) | one bundled blob per world, via existing `readSave`/`writeSave` |
| `minecraft_audio_v1` | `AudioSettings` | **global, untouched** |
| `minecraft_skin_v1` | `SkinSettings` | legacy — read once during migration, then goes stale |
| `minecraft_save_v7` | `SaveData` | legacy — consumed once during migration |

```ts
// lib/game/profiles.ts
type Profile = { id: string; name: string; skinId: SkinId; createdAt: number };
type ProfilesManifest = { version: 1; profiles: Profile[]; activeProfileId: string | null };

// lib/game/worlds.ts
type WorldMeta = {
  id: string; profileId: string;        // the 1:N link
  name: string; seed: number;
  worldgenVersion: number;              // 7 today (see below)
  createdAt: number; lastPlayedAt: number;
};
type WorldsManifest = { version: 1; worlds: WorldMeta[] };
const WORLD_SAVE_PREFIX = "minecraft_world_save_";
function worldSaveKey(id: string) { return WORLD_SAVE_PREFIX + id; }
```

Both manifests use a `{ version: 1, ... }` envelope so they can migrate later, mirroring
`SaveData.version`. Readers are **total** (never throw): drop malformed entries, coerce
`skinId` via `isSkinId`, and repair `activeProfileId` if it points at a deleted profile.

**Seed:** resolved once at `createWorld(profileId, name, seedInput)` and stored on
`WorldMeta.seed` (the manifest becomes the source of truth). Empty input → random in the
same `Math.floor(Math.random()*2147483647)` range the engine uses today; numeric input →
clamped int; non-numeric text → stable hash (Minecraft-style). A fresh world boots with
`new GameEngine({ seed })`; a returning world with `new GameEngine({ save: readSave(key) })`.

**`worldgenVersion` (do NOT bake `_v7` into the key):** keep the per-world save key
version-free. Store `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (= 7 today, new `config.ts` constant) on each
world. The historical `SAVE_KEY` bumps that invalidated block-diff baselines become a
per-world check: if `world.worldgenVersion !== WORLDGEN_VERSION` at load, discard that
world's blob and reboot from its stored seed. Same semantics as today's `_v7` bump, but
per-world and with no key migration.

## Legacy save migration

New `lib/game/legacyMigration.ts` → `migrateLegacySave(storage = localStorage)`, called
once from the menu shell on first mount. **Idempotent**, gated on the *profiles manifest's
absence* (not the legacy save's presence — survives rollback):

1. If `minecraft_profiles_v1` exists → return (already migrated).
2. Read legacy `minecraft_save_v7` (runs the v1→v5 chain) and `minecraft_skin_v1`.
3. `createProfile("Player", legacySkin)` → becomes active.
4. If a legacy save exists: `createWorld(profile.id, "My World", String(seed))` then
   **copy** the blob to `worldSaveKey(world.id)` (copy, not move — a quota failure can't
   lose data), then `removeItem("minecraft_save_v7")` after the copy succeeds.
5. No legacy save (brand-new player): just the default profile, zero worlds.

Leave `minecraft_skin_v1` in place (harmless; per-profile skin now owns appearance).

## React shell / menu state machine

Split `app/page.tsx` so a new **menu shell** sits *above* `MinecraftGame` — the game
component stays "the running game for one world" and its big lifecycle `useEffect` is
untouched.

- **New `components/GameShell.tsx`** owns the screen machine + active profile/world +
  a `playNonce`, runs `migrateLegacySave()` once, and renders the game subtree.
  ```ts
  type Screen =
    | { name: "profile-select" }
    | { name: "world-select"; profileId: string }
    | { name: "play"; profileId: string; worldId: string };
  ```
  Default landing: `profile-select` (active profile highlighted). Flows: pick profile →
  world-select; pick world → `touchWorld(id)` then play; create/rename/delete profile &
  world stay on their screens; "Back" world→profile.

- **Runtime world switch with NO reload — the `key` trick.** Render play as:
  ```tsx
  <MinecraftGame
    key={`${screen.worldId}:${playNonce}`}   // remounts → effect cleanup disposes old engine/renderer
    worldId={screen.worldId} world={world} profile={activeProfile}
    onQuitToWorlds={...} onReloadWorld={() => setPlayNonce(n => n + 1)}
  />
  ```
  Changing `worldId` (or bumping `playNonce`) unmounts the subtree, which runs the existing
  cleanup in `useMinecraftGame.ts:258-273` (disposes renderer/audio/input, clears rAF +
  autosave interval, removes `beforeunload`). A fresh mount boots the new world. This is
  the central move — **zero engine changes**.

- **New menu components** under `components/menu/`: `ProfileSelect.tsx`,
  `WorldSelect.tsx` (worlds for active profile, sorted by `lastPlayedAt`),
  `CreateProfileForm.tsx` (name + skin swatch grid, reusing `SKIN_PRESETS` /
  `skinPortraitUrl` exactly as `PauseMenu` does), `CreateWorldForm.tsx` (name + optional
  seed). Style with existing tokens (`.mc-button`, `.pause-overlay`); add `.menu-screen`/
  `.profile-card`/`.world-card` to a new `app/menu.css` imported after `ui.css` in
  `app/layout.tsx`. Zero binary assets — portraits use the generated `skinPortraitUrl`.

## Wiring `useMinecraftGame` to the selected world + profile

`useMinecraftGame(opts: { worldId; world: WorldMeta; profile; onQuitToWorlds; onReloadWorld })`,
threaded through `MinecraftGame`. Capture `saveKeyRef = worldSaveKey(opts.worldId)` (stable
for the mount's life). Changes in `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts`:

- **`persistGame(api, saveKey, onMessage)`** (currently hardcodes `SAVE_KEY` at line 71) —
  add the key param.
- **`attachMount`** (130): `new GameEngine({ save: readSave(saveKeyRef.current) ?? undefined, seed: opts.world.seed })` — engine prefers `save.seed`, falls back to `seed` for a fresh world.
- **autosave + `beforeunload`** (187-189) and **`saveNow`** (331): use `saveKeyRef.current`.
- **`loadNow`** (334): no reload — guard on `readSave(saveKeyRef.current)`, else call `onReloadWorld()` (nonce bump → remount re-reads disk).
- **`resetNow`** (342): `removeItem(saveKeyRef.current)` then `onReloadWorld()` — fresh mount finds no save, reboots from the stored seed (world keeps its name/seed/identity; this is "Reset World", not delete).
- **Skin from profile**: replace `readSkinSettings()` (100) with `opts.profile.skinId`; `updateSkin` (116) calls `setProfileSkin(opts.profile.id, id)` instead of `writeSkinSettings`, still `setPlayerSkin(...)`.
- **New `quitToWorlds()`**: `persistGame(engine, saveKeyRef.current, flashMessage)` *then* `opts.onQuitToWorlds()` — the autosave interval is cleared on unmount and `beforeunload` doesn't fire on in-app nav, so the quit must save synchronously.
- **Audio unchanged** — stays global.

`components/game/PauseMenu.tsx`: add a **"Quit to Worlds"** button (new `onQuitToWorlds`
prop) next to the existing Save/Load/Reset; skin grid now edits the active profile.

## Files

**New:** `lib/game/manifest.ts` (versioned read/write envelope), `lib/game/profiles.ts`,
`lib/game/worlds.ts`, `lib/game/legacyMigration.ts`, `components/GameShell.tsx`,
`components/menu/{ProfileSelect,WorldSelect,CreateProfileForm,CreateWorldForm}.tsx`,
`app/menu.css`.

**Modified:** `app/page.tsx` (render `GameShell`), `app/layout.tsx` (import menu.css),
`components/MinecraftGame.tsx` (accept/pass props), `lib/game/useMinecraftGame.ts` (above),
`components/game/PauseMenu.tsx` (Quit to Worlds), `lib/game/config.ts`
(`WORLDGEN_VERSION`; mark `SAVE_KEY` as legacy/migration-only).

**Reuse (don't reinvent):** `readSave`/`writeSave` (`lib/game/save.ts:75,92`),
`GameEngine` `{ save, seed }` precedence (`GameEngine.ts:88`), `SKIN_PRESETS` /
`isSkinId` / `skinPortraitUrl` (`playerSkins.ts`), the existing dispose-on-unmount cleanup.

## Edge cases

- **Delete active world:** only reachable from `world-select` (not while playing). On any world delete, also `removeItem(worldSaveKey(id))`.
- **Delete profile with N worlds:** cascade — remove each world's blob + manifest entry *before* removing the profile.
- **Last world / last profile gone:** empty world-select state ("Create your first world"); if zero profiles, route to "create first profile" (`activeProfileId = null`, no profile-less play).
- **Autosave to the right key after a switch:** guaranteed — old interval is cleared in cleanup before the new mount installs its own reading the new `saveKeyRef`.
- **Pointer lock:** menus are plain DOM with no canvas; the "Double-click to play" hint only renders inside `play`; cleanup already runs `exitPointerLock()` so the cursor is free in menus.
- **StrictMode double-mount (dev):** migration is idempotent, so safe.

## Testing & verification

**Unit** (co-located `*.test.ts`, injectable `fakeStorage()` per `skinSettings.test.ts`):
`profiles.test.ts` (CRUD, corrupt-manifest fallback, `activeProfileId` repair),
`worlds.test.ts` (seed resolution: empty/numeric/text-hash/clamp; `worldsForProfile`
filter+sort; `worldSaveKey`; delete), `legacyMigration.test.ts` (all branches +
idempotency + blob copied with matching seed + legacy key removed + audio untouched).

**Component** (`*.test.tsx`): `ProfileSelect`/`WorldSelect`/`CreateWorldForm` (lists,
callbacks, empty states), extend `PauseMenu.test.tsx` (Quit to Worlds fires; add prop to
`renderMenu` defaults), optional `GameShell.test.tsx` with a mocked `MinecraftGame`
asserting the `key` changes on switch.

**E2E** (`e2e/*.e2e.ts`): new `menu.e2e.ts` — fresh storage → profile-select → create
profile → create world (fixed seed) → play, assert `window.__monecraft` boots with that
seed; pause → Quit to Worlds → re-enter, seed persists; create a 2nd world, switch, assert
`__monecraft.engine.state.world.seed` changes **with no page navigation**. Update
`e2e/helpers.ts` to pre-seed a default profile+world via `addInitScript` so existing
gameplay tests still land in play. Update `e2e/smoke.e2e.ts` — its three hardcoded
`minecraft_save_v7` / `minecraft_skin_v1` literals must move to the per-world key /
profile skin.

**Verify gate** (must be green before landing): `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`,
`bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build`; `bun run test:e2e` (renderer/input/
shell touched). Manual pass: create two profiles, create worlds under each, switch worlds
without reload, confirm autosave lands in the right blob, reset a world, delete a world and
a profile, confirm a pre-existing `minecraft_save_v7` migrates into "Player / My World".

**Docs (ship with the change):** `docs/save-format.md` (manifests, per-world key, legacy
migration, `worldgenVersion` discard rule — primary), `docs/architecture.md` (GameShell →
`key` remount lifecycle), `docs/manual.md` + `docs/reference.md` (menus, seed entry),
`docs/testing.md` (e2e boots through menus), `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

## Commit slicing (Conventional Commits; lint/build green each step)

1. `feat(save): add versioned profile + world manifests` — `manifest/profiles/worlds.ts`, `WORLDGEN_VERSION`, unit tests. Pure data layer, app still on legacy save.
2. `feat(save): migrate the legacy single save into a default profile + world` — `legacyMigration.ts` + test, not yet UI-wired.
3. `refactor(game): parameterize useMinecraftGame by world + profile` — thread props, per-world key, skin-from-profile; `app/page.tsx` shim still boots one world; update e2e helpers.
4. `feat(menu): add GameShell state machine and menu screens` — shell + menu components + CSS, `key` remount, Quit to Worlds, `loadNow`/`resetNow` nonce rewrite; component tests.
5. `test(e2e): boot through the menu and cover world switching` — helpers + smoke updates, new `menu.e2e.ts`.
6. `docs: document profiles, worlds, and the save-key model`.

```

1	/**
2	 * Player skin presets — palettes for the third-person body model and the
3	 * pause-menu portraits. Pure data: the renderer applies a palette to the
4	 * model's materials and lib/ui derives a pixel bust from the same colors, so
5	 * a preset is automatically consistent everywhere it appears.
6	 */
7	
8	export type PlayerPalette = {
9	  skin: number;
10	  hair: number;
11	  shirt: number;
12	  pants: number;
13	  shoes: number;

63	export const DEFAULT_PLAYER_PALETTE: PlayerPalette = SKIN_PRESETS[0].palette;

65	export function isSkinId(value: unknown): value is SkinId {
66	  return typeof value === "string" && SKIN_PRESETS.some((preset) => preset.id === value);
67	}

1	import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
2	import { DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS, readSkinSettings, SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY, writeSkinSettings } from "./skinSettings";
3	
4	function fakeStorage(initial: Record<string, string> = {}): Storage {
5	  const map = new Map(Object.entries(initial));
6	  return {
7	    get length() {
8	      return map.size;
9	    },
10	    clear: () => map.clear(),
11	    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,
12	    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,
13	    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),
14	    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void map.set(key, value)
15	  };
16	}
17	
18	describe("skin settings persistence", () => {
19	  test("round-trips through storage", () => {
20	    const storage = fakeStorage();
21	    writeSkinSettings({ skinId: "robot" }, storage);
22	    expect(readSkinSettings(storage)).toEqual({ skinId: "robot" });
23	  });
24	
25	  test("missing or corrupt data falls back to the default skin", () => {
26	    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage())).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);
27	    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: "not json{" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);
28	    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: "null" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);
29	  });
30	
31	  test("unknown or wrong-typed skin ids are sanitized to the default", () => {
32	    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ skinId: "herobrine" }) }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);
33	    expect(readSkinSettings(fakeStorage({ [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ skinId: 7 }) }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_SKIN_SETTINGS);
34	  });
35	
36	  test("write failures are swallowed", () => {
37	    const storage = fakeStorage();
38	    storage.setItem = () => {
39	      throw new Error("quota");
40	    };
41	    expect(() => writeSkinSettings({ skinId: "alex" }, storage)).not.toThrow();
42	  });
43	});
44

Bumped whenever worldgen\n// changes, so old block-diffs (which index against generated terrain) can't be\n// applied to a different baseline: v6 added dungeons; v7 added deep-cave lava\n// lakes. Each world records the WORLDGEN_VERSION it was generated under; a world\n// whose recorded version differs from this constant has its block-diffs\n// discarded and is rebooted from its stored seed (lib/game/worlds.ts). {
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/manifest.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Shared helpers for the versioned localStorage manifests (profiles, worlds).\n *\n * Each manifest is a `{ version, ...payload }` object living in its own key.\n * Reads must be *total* — a corrupt or absent blob falls back to a default,\n * never throws — and writes swallow quota/privacy-mode failures the same way\n * the audio and skin preferences do, so a failed persist never crashes the\n * game. Storage is injectable so the manifest modules can be unit-tested\n * without a browser.\n */\n\n/** Per-call dependencies for manifest mutations — injectable for deterministic tests. */\nexport type ManifestDeps = {\n  storage?: Storage;\n  /** Wall clock for `createdAt`/`lastPlayedAt`; defaults to `Date.now`. */\n  now?: () => number;\n  /** Stable id generator; defaults to `crypto.randomUUID` with a random fallback. */\n  uid?: () => string;\n};\n\n/** Resolves a `ManifestDeps` to concrete functions, applying the defaults. */\nexport function resolveDeps(deps: ManifestDeps = {}): { storage: Storage; now: () => number; uid: () => string } {\n  return {\n    storage: deps.storage ?? localStorage,\n    now: deps.now ?? (() => Date.now()),\n    uid: deps.uid ?? genId\n  };\n}\n\n/** Reads and JSON-parses a manifest key, returning null on absence or any parse error. */\nexport function readManifestRaw(key: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): unknown {\n  try {\n    const raw = storage.getItem(key);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    return JSON.parse(raw) as unknown;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** Persists a manifest value, swallowing quota/privacy-mode failures. */\nexport function writeManifest(key: string, value: unknown, storage: Storage = localStorage): void {\n  try {\n    storage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));\n  } catch {\n    // Quota/privacy-mode failures lose the manifest write, never the game.\n  }\n}\n\n/** A stable unique id — `crypto.randomUUID` when available, otherwise a random string. */\nexport function genId(): string {\n  if (typeof crypto !== \"undefined\" && typeof crypto.randomUUID === \"function\") {\n    return crypto.randomUUID();\n  }\n  return `id-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`;\n}\n\n/** Trims, collapses inner whitespace, and clamps a user-entered name to `max` chars. */\nexport function sanitizeName(value: unknown, fallback: string, max: number): string {\n  if (typeof value !== \"string\") return fallback;\n  const cleaned = value.trim().replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").slice(0, max);\n  return cleaned.length > 0 ? cleaned : fallback;\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/profiles.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Player profiles — the top of the save hierarchy. A profile is a distinct\n * player (name + appearance) that owns a list of worlds (see lib/game/worlds.ts\n * for the 1:N link via `WorldMeta.profileId`). Profiles carry identity only;\n * audio settings stay global.\n *\n * The whole list lives in one versioned localStorage blob. Readers are total:\n * malformed entries are dropped, skin ids are sanitized, and a dangling\n * `activeProfileId` is repaired to the first surviving profile (or null).\n */\n\nimport { DEFAULT_SKIN_ID, isSkinId, type SkinId } from \"./playerSkins\";\nimport { readManifestRaw, resolveDeps, sanitizeName, writeManifest, type ManifestDeps } from \"./manifest\";\n\nexport type Profile = {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  skinId: SkinId;\n  createdAt: number;\n};\n\nexport type ProfilesManifest = {\n  version: 1;\n  profiles: Profile[];\n  /** The profile to preselect on boot; null shows the create-first-profile flow. */\n  activeProfileId: string | null;\n};\n\nexport const PROFILES_KEY = \"minecraft_profiles_v1\";\nexport const MAX_PROFILE_NAME = 24;\nexport const DEFAULT_PROFILE_NAME = \"Player\";\n\nexport const DEFAULT_PROFILES_MANIFEST: ProfilesManifest = { version: 1, profiles: [], activeProfileId: null };\n\n/** Coerces one raw entry into a valid Profile, or null if it can't be salvaged. */\nfunction sanitizeProfile(raw: unknown): Profile | null {\n  if (!raw || typeof raw !== \"object\") return null;\n  const entry = raw as Record<string, unknown>;\n  if (typeof entry.id !== \"string\" || entry.id.length === 0) return null;\n  return {\n    id: entry.id,\n    name: sanitizeName(entry.name, DEFAULT_PROFILE_NAME, MAX_PROFILE_NAME),\n    skinId: isSkinId(entry.skinId) ? entry.skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID,\n    createdAt: typeof entry.createdAt === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(entry.createdAt) ? entry.createdAt : 0\n  };\n}\n\n/** Repairs `activeProfileId` so it always names a present profile (or null when empty). */\nfunction withRepairedActive(profiles: Profile[], activeProfileId: unknown): ProfilesManifest {\n  const valid = typeof activeProfileId === \"string\" && profiles.some((p) => p.id === activeProfileId);\n  return {\n    version: 1,\n    profiles,\n    activeProfileId: valid ? (activeProfileId as string) : (profiles[0]?.id ?? null)\n  };\n}\n\n/** Reads the profiles manifest, dropping malformed entries and repairing the active id. Never throws. */\nexport function readProfiles(storage: Storage = localStorage): ProfilesManifest {\n  const raw = readManifestRaw(PROFILES_KEY, storage) as Partial<ProfilesManifest> | null;\n  if (!raw || !Array.isArray(raw.profiles)) return { ...DEFAULT_PROFILES_MANIFEST };\n  const profiles = raw.profiles.map(sanitizeProfile).filter((p): p is Profile => p !== null);\n  return withRepairedActive(profiles, raw.activeProfileId);\n}\n\nexport function getProfile(id: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): Profile | null {\n  return readProfiles(storage).profiles.find((p) => p.id === id) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport function getActiveProfile(storage: Storage = localStorage): Profile | null {\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  return manifest.profiles.find((p) => p.id === manifest.activeProfileId) ?? null;\n}\n\n/** Creates a profile, makes it the active one, persists, and returns it. */\nexport function createProfile(name: string, skinId: SkinId, deps: ManifestDeps = {}): Profile {\n  const { storage, now, uid } = resolveDeps(deps);\n  const profile: Profile = {\n    id: uid(),\n    name: sanitizeName(name, DEFAULT_PROFILE_NAME, MAX_PROFILE_NAME),\n    skinId: isSkinId(skinId) ? skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID,\n    createdAt: now()\n  };\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  const next: ProfilesManifest = { version: 1, profiles: [...manifest.profiles, profile], activeProfileId: profile.id };\n  writeManifest(PROFILES_KEY, next, storage);\n  return profile;\n}\n\nexport function renameProfile(id: string, name: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): ProfilesManifest {\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  const next = withRepairedActive(\n    manifest.profiles.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, name: sanitizeName(name, p.name, MAX_PROFILE_NAME) } : p)),\n    manifest.activeProfileId\n  );\n  writeManifest(PROFILES_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n\nexport function setProfileSkin(id: string, skinId: SkinId, storage: Storage = localStorage): ProfilesManifest {\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  const safeSkin = isSkinId(skinId) ? skinId : DEFAULT_SKIN_ID;\n  const next = withRepairedActive(\n    manifest.profiles.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, skinId: safeSkin } : p)),\n    manifest.activeProfileId\n  );\n  writeManifest(PROFILES_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n\nexport function setActiveProfile(id: string | null, storage: Storage = localStorage): ProfilesManifest {\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  const next = withRepairedActive(manifest.profiles, id);\n  writeManifest(PROFILES_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n\n/**\n * Removes a profile and repairs the active id. Does NOT touch the profile's\n * worlds — call `deleteWorldsForProfile` (lib/game/worlds.ts) first so the\n * world records and their save blobs are reclaimed too.\n */\nexport function deleteProfile(id: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): ProfilesManifest {\n  const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n  const profiles = manifest.profiles.filter((p) => p.id !== id);\n  const next = withRepairedActive(profiles, manifest.activeProfileId === id ? null : manifest.activeProfileId);\n  writeManifest(PROFILES_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/worlds.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Worlds — the second level of the save hierarchy. Each world belongs to one\n * profile (`profileId`) and owns a single bundled SaveData blob under its own\n * `minecraft_world_save_<id>` key (read/written with the existing\n * lib/game/save.ts helpers). The world *index* — names, seeds, ordering — lives\n * in one versioned manifest blob; the heavy per-world saves stay separate so\n * the index reads cheaply.\n *\n * A world's `seed` is resolved once at creation and is the source of truth for\n * regeneration; `worldgenVersion` records the WORLDGEN_VERSION it was generated\n * under so a future baseline bump can discard its stale block-diffs.\n */\n\nimport { WORLDGEN_VERSION } from \"./config\";\nimport { readManifestRaw, resolveDeps, sanitizeName, writeManifest, type ManifestDeps } from \"./manifest\";\n\nexport type WorldMeta = {\n  id: string;\n  profileId: string;\n  name: string;\n  seed: number;\n  worldgenVersion: number;\n  createdAt: number;\n  lastPlayedAt: number;\n};\n\nexport type WorldsManifest = {\n  version: 1;\n  worlds: WorldMeta[];\n};\n\nexport const WORLDS_KEY = \"minecraft_worlds_v1\";\nexport const WORLD_SAVE_PREFIX = \"minecraft_world_save_\";\nexport const MAX_WORLD_NAME = 32;\nexport const DEFAULT_WORLD_NAME = \"New World\";\nexport const MAX_SEED = 2147483647;\n\nexport const DEFAULT_WORLDS_MANIFEST: WorldsManifest = { version: 1, worlds: [] };\n\n/** The per-world SaveData key handed to readSave/writeSave. */\nexport function worldSaveKey(worldId: string): string {\n  return WORLD_SAVE_PREFIX + worldId;\n}\n\n/** FNV-1a hash of a text seed into a stable non-negative 31-bit integer (Minecraft-style). */\nexport function hashStringToSeed(text: string): number {\n  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;\n  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {\n    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(i);\n    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193);\n  }\n  return Math.abs(hash | 0) % MAX_SEED;\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolves a user seed input to an integer in 0..MAX_SEED-1: blank → random\n * (same entropy the engine used for a fresh world), numeric text → clamped int,\n * any other text → stable hash. `rng` is injectable for deterministic tests.\n */\nexport function resolveSeed(seedInput: string | null, rng: () => number = Math.random): number {\n  const trimmed = (seedInput ?? \"\").trim();\n  if (trimmed === \"\") return Math.floor(rng() * MAX_SEED);\n  const asNumber = Number(trimmed);\n  if (Number.isFinite(asNumber)) return Math.floor(Math.abs(asNumber)) % MAX_SEED;\n  return hashStringToSeed(trimmed);\n}\n\n/** Coerces one raw entry into a valid WorldMeta, or null if it can't be salvaged. */\nfunction sanitizeWorld(raw: unknown): WorldMeta | null {\n  if (!raw || typeof raw !== \"object\") return null;\n  const entry = raw as Record<string, unknown>;\n  if (typeof entry.id !== \"string\" || entry.id.length === 0) return null;\n  if (typeof entry.profileId !== \"string\" || entry.profileId.length === 0) return null;\n  if (typeof entry.seed !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(entry.seed)) return null;\n  const num = (value: unknown, fallback: number) => (typeof value === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : fallback);\n  return {\n    id: entry.id,\n    profileId: entry.profileId,\n    name: sanitizeName(entry.name, DEFAULT_WORLD_NAME, MAX_WORLD_NAME),\n    seed: Math.floor(entry.seed),\n    worldgenVersion: num(entry.worldgenVersion, WORLDGEN_VERSION),\n    createdAt: num(entry.createdAt, 0),\n    lastPlayedAt: num(entry.lastPlayedAt, 0)\n  };\n}\n\n/** Reads the worlds manifest, dropping malformed entries. Never throws. */\nexport function readWorlds(storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldsManifest {\n  const raw = readManifestRaw(WORLDS_KEY, storage) as Partial<WorldsManifest> | null;\n  if (!raw || !Array.isArray(raw.worlds)) return { ...DEFAULT_WORLDS_MANIFEST };\n  return { version: 1, worlds: raw.worlds.map(sanitizeWorld).filter((w): w is WorldMeta => w !== null) };\n}\n\nexport function getWorld(id: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldMeta | null {\n  return readWorlds(storage).worlds.find((w) => w.id === id) ?? null;\n}\n\n/** A profile's worlds, most-recently-played first (ties broken by creation time). */\nexport function worldsForProfile(profileId: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldMeta[] {\n  return readWorlds(storage)\n    .worlds.filter((w) => w.profileId === profileId)\n    .sort((a, b) => b.lastPlayedAt - a.lastPlayedAt || b.createdAt - a.createdAt);\n}\n\n/** Creates a world for a profile, persists it, and returns it. `deps.rng` seeds blank-input worlds. */\nexport function createWorld(\n  profileId: string,\n  name: string,\n  seedInput: string | null,\n  deps: ManifestDeps & { rng?: () => number } = {}\n): WorldMeta {\n  const { storage, now, uid } = resolveDeps(deps);\n  const createdAt = now();\n  const world: WorldMeta = {\n    id: uid(),\n    profileId,\n    name: sanitizeName(name, DEFAULT_WORLD_NAME, MAX_WORLD_NAME),\n    seed: resolveSeed(seedInput, deps.rng ?? Math.random),\n    worldgenVersion: WORLDGEN_VERSION,\n    createdAt,\n    lastPlayedAt: createdAt\n  };\n  const manifest = readWorlds(storage);\n  writeManifest(WORLDS_KEY, { version: 1, worlds: [...manifest.worlds, world] }, storage);\n  return world;\n}\n\nexport function renameWorld(id: string, name: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldsManifest {\n  const manifest = readWorlds(storage);\n  const next: WorldsManifest = {\n    version: 1,\n    worlds: manifest.worlds.map((w) => (w.id === id ? { ...w, name: sanitizeName(name, w.name, MAX_WORLD_NAME) } : w))\n  };\n  writeManifest(WORLDS_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n\n/** Bumps a world's `lastPlayedAt` so it sorts to the top of its profile's list. */\nexport function touchWorld(id: string, deps: ManifestDeps = {}): WorldsManifest {\n  const { storage, now } = resolveDeps(deps);\n  const manifest = readWorlds(storage);\n  const next: WorldsManifest = {\n    version: 1,\n    worlds: manifest.worlds.map((w) => (w.id === id ? { ...w, lastPlayedAt: now() } : w))\n  };\n  writeManifest(WORLDS_KEY, next, storage);\n  return next;\n}\n\n/** Removes a world from the index and deletes its save blob. */\nexport function deleteWorld(id: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldsManifest {\n  const manifest = readWorlds(storage);\n  const next: WorldsManifest = { version: 1, worlds: manifest.worlds.filter((w) => w.id !== id) };\n  writeManifest(WORLDS_KEY, next, storage);\n  try {\n    storage.removeItem(worldSaveKey(id));\n  } catch {\n    // A failed blob cleanup just leaves an orphaned key; the index no longer references it.\n  }\n  return next;\n}\n\n/** Cascade for profile deletion: removes every world of a profile and its save blobs. */\nexport function deleteWorldsForProfile(profileId: string, storage: Storage = localStorage): WorldsManifest {\n  const manifest = readWorlds(storage);\n  const doomed = manifest.worlds.filter((w) => w.profileId === profileId);\n  const next: WorldsManifest = { version: 1, worlds: manifest.worlds.filter((w) => w.profileId !== profileId) };\n  writeManifest(WORLDS_KEY, next, storage);\n  for (const world of doomed) {\n    try {\n      storage.removeItem(worldSaveKey(world.id));\n    } catch {\n      // Orphaned blob; the index no longer references it.\n    }\n  }\n  return next;\n}\n"
}

Map(Object.entries(initial));\n  return {\n    get length() {\n      return map.size;\n    },\n    clear: () => map.clear(),\n    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,\n    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),\n    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void map.set(key, value)\n  };\n}\n\n/** Deterministic deps so ids and timestamps are predictable. */\nfunction seqDeps(storage: Storage) {\n  let n = 0;\n  return { storage, now: () => 1000 + n, uid: () => `p${(n += 1)}` };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"profiles manifest\", () => {\n  test(\"create appends and makes the new profile active\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    const a = createProfile(\"Alice\", \"alex\", seqDeps(storage));\n \"Bob\"]);\n    expect(manifest.activeProfileId).toBe(b.id);\n    expect(a.skinId).toBe(\"alex\");\n  });\n\n  test(\"missing or corrupt manifest falls back to the default\", () => {\n    expect(readProfiles(fakeStorage())).toEqual(DEFAULT_PROFILES_MANIFEST);\n    expect(readProfiles(fakeStorage({ [PROFILES_KEY]: \"not json{\" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_PROFILES_MANIFEST);\n    expect(readProfiles(fakeStorage({ [PROFILES_KEY]: \"null\" }))).toEqual(DEFAULT_PROFILES_MANIFEST);\n  });\n\n  test(\"malformed entries are dropped and skin ids sanitized\", () => {\n    const raw = \"default\" });\n  });\n\n  test(\"dangling activeProfileId is repaired to the first profile\", () => {\n    const raw = JSON.stringify({\n      version: 1,\n      profiles: [{ id: \"p1\", name: \"First\", skinId: \"default\", createdAt: 1 }],\n      activeProfileId: \"ghost\"\n    });\n    expect(readProfiles(fakeStorage({ [PROFILES_KEY]: raw })).activeProfileId).toBe(\"p1\");\n  });\n\n  test(\"rename and setProfileSkin mutate the named profile\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    const p = createProfile(\"Old\", \"default\", seqDeps(storage));\n    renameProfile(p.id, \"  New   Name  \", storage);\n    setProfileSkin(p.id, \"knight\", storage);\n    const updated = getActiveProfile(storage);\n    expect(updated).toMatchObject({ name: \"New Name\", skinId: \"knight\" });\n  });\n\n  test(\"delete removes a profile and clears active when it was the one deleted\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    const a = createProfile(\"A\", \"default\", { storage, uid: () => \"pa\" });\n    const b = createProfile(\"B\", \"default\", { storage, uid: () => \"pb\" });\n    setActiveProfile(a.id, storage);\n    deleteProfile(a.id, storage);\n    const manifest = readProfiles(storage);\n    expect(manifest.profiles.map((p) => p.id)).toEqual([b.id]);\n    expect(manifest.activeProfileId).toBe(b.id); // repaired to the survivor\n  });\n\n  test(\"write failures are swallowed\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    storage.setItem = () => {\n      throw new Error(\"quota\");\n    };\n    expect(() => createProfile(\"Q\", \"default\", { storage })).not.toThrow();\n  });\n});\n"

map.clear(),\n    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,\n    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),\n    setItem: expect(resolveSeed(\"hello\")).toBe(hashStringToSeed(\"hello\"));\n    expect(resolveSeed(\"hello\")).toBe(resolveSeed(\"hello\"));\n \"b1\" });\n    storage.setItem(worldSaveKey(\"a1\"), \"{}\");\n    storage.setItem(worldSaveKey(\"a2\"), \"{}\");\n    deleteWorldsForProfile(\"A\", storage);\n    expect(readWorlds(storage).worlds.map((w) => w.id)).toEqual([\"b1\"]);\n    expect(storage.getItem(worldSaveKey(\"a1\"))).toBeNull();\n    expect(storage.getItem(worldSaveKey(\"a2\"))).toBeNull();\n {
  "command": "git add lib/game/manifest.ts lib/game/profiles.ts lib/game/worlds.ts lib/game/profiles.test.ts lib/game/worlds.test.ts lib/game/config.ts && git commit -q -F - <<'EOF'\nfeat(save): add versioned profile and world manifests\n\nIntroduce the data layer for multiple worlds and player profiles. Two\nversioned localStorage manifests sit above the existing per-world save\nblobs:\n\n- profiles.ts: a profile is a player identity (name + skin) that owns a\n  list of worlds. Readers are total — malformed entries are dropped, skin\n  ids sanitized, and a dangling activeProfileId repaired to a real one.\n- worlds.ts: each world records its profileId, name, a seed resolved once\n  at creation (blank -> random, numeric -> clamped, text -> stable FNV\n  hash), and the WORLDGEN_VERSION it was generated under. The heavy\n  SaveData stays in its own minecraft_world_save_<id> key.\n- manifest.ts: shared read-guard / swallow-on-write / injectable-deps\n  helpers so both modules stay declarative and unit-testable.\n\nconfig.ts gains WORLDGEN_VERSION (the worldgen baseline, today 7) so a\nfuture baseline bump can discard a single world's stale block-diffs\nper-world instead of wiping every save via a SAVE_KEY rename. SAVE_KEY is\nnow legacy: only the upcoming migration reads it.\n\nPure data layer with no consumers yet; the app still boots the legacy\nsingle save. Covered by profiles.test.ts and worlds.test.ts.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",
  "description": "Commit data-layer slice"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/legacyMigration.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * One-time migration from the single-world era into the profile/world model.\n *\n * Before this feature the game kept one world under `minecraft_save_v7` and a\n * global skin under `minecraft_skin_v1`. On first boot after the feature lands,\n * we fold those into a default \"Player\" profile (carrying the old skin) plus a\n * single \"My World\" pointing at a copy of the old save blob.\n *\n * Idempotent and gated on the *profiles manifest's absence* — its presence is\n * the \"already migrated\" flag, so this survives a rollback that re-creates the\n * legacy key. Storage and the createdAt/id sources are injectable for tests.\n */\n\nimport { SAVE_KEY } from \"./config\";\nimport { resolveDeps, type ManifestDeps } from \"./manifest\";\nimport { createProfile, PROFILES_KEY } from \"./profiles\";\nimport { readSave, writeSave } from \"./save\";\nimport { readSkinSettings } from \"./skinSettings\";\nimport { createWorld, worldSaveKey } from \"./worlds\";\n\nexport const LEGACY_WORLD_NAME = \"My World\";\n\nexport function migrateLegacySave(deps: ManifestDeps = {}): void {\n  const { storage } = resolveDeps(deps);\n\n  // Presence of the profiles manifest means the feature has already run here.\n  if (storage.getItem(PROFILES_KEY) !== null) return;\n\n  const legacy = readSave(SAVE_KEY, storage); // runs the v1->v5 migration chain\n  const { skinId } = readSkinSettings(storage); // old global skin seeds the default profile\n\n  const profile = createProfile(\"Player\", skinId, deps); // writes the manifest, becomes active\n\n  if (!legacy) return; // brand-new player: just the default profile, no worlds yet\n\n  const world = createWorld(profile.id, LEGACY_WORLD_NAME, String(legacy.seed), deps);\n  try {\n    // Copy (not move) so a mid-migration failure can never lose the original.\n    writeSave(worldSaveKey(world.id), legacy, storage);\n    try {\n      storage.removeItem(SAVE_KEY);\n    } catch {\n      // Leaving the legacy key just wastes a little space; the copy is authoritative.\n    }\n  } catch {\n    // Quota failure copying the blob (storage briefly holds both): the world\n    // record still exists and will boot fresh from its stored seed; the legacy\n    // key is left intact rather than risk losing the original edits.\n  }\n}\n"
}

Storage {\n  const map = new Map(Object.entries(initial));\n  return {\n    get length() {\n      return map.size;\n    },\n    clear: () => map.clear(),\n    getItem: (key: string) => map.get(key) ?? null,\n    key: (index: number) => [...map.keys()][index] ?? null,\n    removeItem: (key: string) => void map.delete(key),\n    setItem: (key: string, value: string) => void map.set(key, value)\n  };\n}\n\nconst LEGACY_SAVE: SaveData = { version: 5, seed: 12345, changes: [[7, 3]], player: { x: 1, y: 2, z: 3 } } as SaveData;\n\n/** Deterministic ids so the profile and world get distinct, predictable keys. */\nfunction seqDeps(storage: Storage) {\n  let n = 0;\n  return { storage, now: () => 100 + n, uid: () => `id${(n += 1)}` };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"legacy save migration\", () => {\n  test(\"folds a legacy save + global skin into a default profile and world\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage({\n      [SAVE_KEY]: JSON.stringify(LEGACY_SAVE),\n      [SKIN_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ skinId: \"knight\" }),\n      [AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY]: JSON.stringify({ master: 0.4, music: 0.2, muted: true })\n    });\n\n    migrateLegacySave(seqDeps(storage));\n\n    const profile = getActiveProfile(storage);\n    expect(profile).toMatchObject({ name: \"Player\", skinId: \"knight\" });\n\n    const worlds = worldsForProfile(profile!.id, storage);\n    expect(worlds).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(worlds[0]).toMatchObject({ name: LEGACY_WORLD_NAME, seed: 12345 });\n\n    // The blob is copied to the per-world key and the legacy key is removed.\n    expect(readSave(worldSaveKey(worlds[0].id), storage)).toMatchObject({ seed: 12345, changes: [[7, 3]] });\n    expect(storage.getItem(SAVE_KEY)).toBeNull();\n\n    // Audio settings are global and untouched.\n    expect(storage.getItem(AUDIO_SETTINGS_KEY)).toBe(JSON.stringify({ master: 0.4, music: 0.2, muted: true }));\n  });\n\n  test(\"brand-new player gets only a default profile, no worlds\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage();\n    migrateLegacySave(seqDeps(storage));\n    const profile = getActiveProfile(storage);\n    expect(profile).toMatchObject({ name: \"Player\", skinId: \"default\" });\n    expect(readWorlds(storage).worlds).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n\n  test(\"is idempotent — a second run is a no-op\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage({ [SAVE_KEY]: JSON.stringify(LEGACY_SAVE) });\n    migrateLegacySave(seqDeps(storage));\n    const before = storage.getItem(PROFILES_KEY);\n    const worldsBefore = readWorlds(storage).worlds;\n\n    migrateLegacySave(seqDeps(storage)); // profiles manifest already present\n    expect(storage.getItem(PROFILES_KEY)).toBe(before);\n    expect(readProfiles(storage).profiles).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(readWorlds(storage).worlds).toEqual(worldsBefore);\n  });\n\n  test(\"no custom skin falls back to the default\", () => {\n    const storage = fakeStorage({ [SAVE_KEY]: JSON.stringify(LEGACY_SAVE) });\n    migrateLegacySave(seqDeps(storage));\n    expect(getActiveProfile(storage)?.skinId).toBe(\"default\");\n  });\n});\n"
}

32	/**
33	 * The Minecraft-style game menu: shown while the engine is paused, with the

36	export default function PauseMenu({
37	  saveMessage,
38	  audioSettings,
39	  onAudioSettingsChange,

41	  onSkinChange,
42	  onBack,
43	  onSave,
44	  onLoad,

46	}: PauseMenuProps) {

48	
49	  const volumeSlider = (label: string, value: number, onValue: (volume: number) => void) => (
50	    <label className="pause-sound-row">

52	      <input
53	        className="mc-slider"
54	        type="range"
55	        min={0}
56	        max={100}
57	        value={Math.round(value * 100)}
58	        onChange={(event) => onValue(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(event.target.value) / 100)))}
59	        aria-label={`${label} volume`}
60	      />
61	    </label>
62	  );
63	
64	  return (
65	    <div className="pause-overlay">
66	      <div className="pause-menu">
67	        <div className="pause-title">Game Menu</div>
68	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onBack}>
69	          Back to Game
70	        </button>
71	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onSave}>
72	          Save Game
73	        </button>
74	        <button className="mc-button" onClick={onLoad}>
75	          Load Save
76	        </button>
77	        {confirmingReset ? (

79	            <button className="mc-button danger" onClick={onReset}>
80	              Confirm Reset
81	            </button>
82	            <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingReset(false)}>
83	              Cancel
84	            </button>
85	          </div>
86	        ) : (
87	          <button className="mc-button" onClick={() => setConfirmingReset(true)}>
88	            Reset World
89	          </button>
90	        )}
91	        <div className="pause-sound">
92	          {volumeSlider("Sound", audioSettings.master, (volume) => onAudioSettingsChange({ master: volume }))}
93	          {volumeSlider("Music", audioSettings.music, (volume) => onAudioSettingsChange({ music: volume }))}
94	          <button className="mc-button" aria-pressed={audioSettings.muted} onClick={() => onAudioSettingsChange({ muted: !audioSettings.muted })}>
95	            {audioSettings.muted ? "Unmute Sound" : "Mute Sound"}
96	          </button>

124	      </div>

66	/**
67	 * The framework-agnostic game core. Owns all simulation state, advances it in
68	 * step(), and accepts player intents through dispatch(). No React, no DOM, no
69	 * rendering — the renderer reads state, the React shell subscribes to
70	 * snapshots via subscribe()/getSnapshot() (useSyncExternalStore compatible).
71	 */
72	export class GameEngine {
73	  readonly state: GameState;
74	  private readonly rng: () => number;
75	  private readonly surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
76	  private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>();
77	  private events: GameEvent[] = [];
78	  private snapshot: GameSnapshot;
79	  // Boss snapshot is rebuilt only when its rounded HP% changes, so the
80	  // ref-equality diff in refreshSnapshot doesn't re-render React every frame.
81	  private lastBoss: { hpPercent: number } | null = null;
82	  private lastBossPercent = -1;
83	
84	  constructor(options: GameEngineOptions = {}) {
85	    const save = options.save ?? null;
86	    this.rng = options.rng ?? Math.random;
87	
88	    const seed = save?.seed ?? options.seed ?? Math.floor(Math.random() * 2147483647);
89	    const size = options.worldSize ?? { x: WORLD_SIZE_X, y: WORLD_SIZE_Y, z: WORLD_SIZE_Z };
90	    const world = new VoxelWorld(size.x, size.y, size.z, seed);
91	    generateWorld(world);
92	    // Re-derive the dungeon chest/spawner positions from the seed (the world is
93	    // regenerated deterministically each load, so these match generation).
94	    const dungeonSites = collectDungeonSites(world);
95	
96	    const blockChanges = createBlockChangeTracker(world);
97	    if (save) blockChanges.applySavedChanges(save.changes);
98	
99	    // Bake per-voxel light now the block grid is final (worldgen + saved edits).
100	    // Derived cache, never serialized — see lighting.ts / docs/save-format.md.
101	    world.light = computeFullLight(world);
102	
103	    this.surfaceYAt = createSurfaceYAt(world);
104	
105	    const firstSpawn = findSpawnOnLand(world, Math.floor(world.sizeX / 2), Math.floor(world.sizeZ / 2));
106	    this.state = {
107	      world,
108	      blockChanges,
109	      player: {
110	        position: new THREE.Vector3(firstSpawn.x, firstSpawn.y, firstSpawn.z),
111	        velocity: new THREE.Vector3(),
112	        yaw: 0,
113	        pitch: 0,
114	        onGround: false
115	      },
116	      inventory: createInitialInventory(),
117	      equippedArmor: createEmptyArmorEquipment(),
118	      selectedSlot: 0,
119	      hearts: MAX_HEARTS,
120	      hunger: MAX_HUNGER,
121	      oxygen: MAX_OXYGEN,
122	      isDead: false,
123	      respawnTimer: 0,
124	      inventoryOpen: false,
125	      craftingStation: null,
126	      containers: new Map(),
127	      openContainerIndex: null,
128	      dungeonChestIndices: new Set(dungeonSites.chestIndices),
129	      dungeonSpawnerIndices: new Set(dungeonSites.spawnerIndices),
130	      lootedDungeonChests: new Set(),
131	      paused: false,
132	      debugOpen: false,
133	      debugInfo: null,
134	      cameraMode: "first",
135	      capsActive: false,
136	      mobs: [],
137	      nextMobId: 1,

3	/**
4	 * Shared E2E plumbing. Tests assert against the live simulation through the
5	 * window.__monecraft debug handle rather than pixels — see docs/testing.md.
6	 */
7	
8	export const test = base.extend<{ gamePage: Page }>({
9	  // A page that has booted the game, with console errors treated as failures.
10	  // (The fixture continuation is named `runTest`, not Playwright's
11	  // conventional `use`, to avoid colliding with React's rules-of-hooks lint.)
12	  gamePage: async ({ page }, runTest) => {
13	    const errors: string[] = [];
14	    page.on("console", (message) => {
15	      if (message.type() !== "error") return;
16	      // The app ships no favicon; Chrome's automatic /favicon.ico 404 is noise.
17	      if (message.text().includes("Failed to load resource") && message.location().url.endsWith("/favicon.ico")) return;
18	      errors.push(`${message.text()} (${message.location().url})`);
19	    });
20	    page.on("pageerror", (error) => errors.push(String(error)));
21	
22	    await page.goto("/");
23	    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft !== undefined, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
24	    // Wait for the first frames so the engine has stepped and the scene drew.
25	    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft!.renderer.renderedTriangles() > 0, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
26	
27	    await runTest(page);
28	
29	    expect(errors, "no console/page errors during the test").toEqual([]);
30	  }
31	});
32	
33	export { expect };
34	
35	/**
36	 * The game boots at dawn with aggro hostiles near the spawn; interactive
37	 * tests clear them and move to midday so knockback can't disrupt input.
38	 */
39	export async function calmDaytime(page: Page): Promise<void> {
40	  await page.evaluate(() => {
41	    const state = window.__monecraft!.engine.state;
42	    state.mobs = state.mobs.filter((mob) => !mob.hostile);
43	    state.dayClock = 60;
44	  });
45	}
46	
47	export async function playerPosition(page: Page): Promise<{ x: number; y: number; z: number }> {
48	  return page.evaluate(() => {
49	    const { position } = window.__monecraft!.engine.state.player;
50	    return { x: position.x, y: position.y, z: position.z };
51	  });
52	}
53	
54	/**
55	 * Double-clicks the canvas to request pointer lock; if the browser refuses
56	 * (headless Chromium cannot engage pointer lock at all), forces the input
57	 * controller's lock flag so everything downstream — keys → engine, held mouse
58	 * → mining — is still exercised for real. The acquisition UX itself stays in
59	 * the manual gameplay pass.
60	 */
61	export async function acquirePointerLock(page: Page): Promise<void> {
62	  const canvas = page.locator(".game-canvas-wrap canvas");
63	  await canvas.dblclick();
64	  try {
65	    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.pointerLockElement !== null, undefined, { timeout: 2000 });
66	  } catch {
67	    await page.evaluate(() => {
68	      window.__monecraft!.input.input.pointerLocked = true;
69	    });
70	  }
71	}
72	
73	export async function itemCount(page: Page, itemId: string): Promise<number> {
74	  return page.evaluate((id) => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.inventory.filter((slot) => slot.id === id).reduce((sum, slot) => sum + slot.count, 0), itemId);
75	}
76	
```

  "new_string": "function persistGame(api: GameApi, saveKey: string, onMessage: (text: string) => void): void {\n  try {\n    writeSave(saveKey, api.serialize());\n    onMessage(\"Saved\");\n  } catch {\n    onMessage(\"Save failed\");\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * One mounted game = one world played by one profile. The owning shell remounts\n * this hook (via a React `key` on the world id) to switch worlds, so the\n * per-world save key and the profile are fixed for the hook's lifetime.\n */\nexport type UseMinecraftGameOptions = {\n  world: WorldMeta;\n  profile: Profile;\n  /** Leave this world and return to the world list (the shell unmounts us). */\n  onQuitToWorlds: () => void;\n  /** Re-read this world from disk by forcing a fresh mount (used by Load/Reset). */\n  onReloadWorld: () => void;\n};\n\nexport function useMinecraftGame(opts: UseMinecraftGameOptions) {\n  // Stable for the mount's life (the shell keys this hook by world id), but kept\n  // in refs so the long-lived rAF/autosave effect always reads the live values.\n  const saveKeyRef = useRef(worldSaveKey(opts.world.id));\n  saveKeyRef.current = worldSaveKey(opts.world.id);\n  const worldSeedRef = useRef(opts.world.seed);\n  worldSeedRef.current = opts.world.seed;"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/app/page.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport MinecraftGame from \"@/components/MinecraftGame\";\nimport { migrateLegacySave } from \"@/lib/game/legacyMigration\";\nimport { createProfile, getActiveProfile, type Profile } from \"@/lib/game/profiles\";\nimport { createWorld, worldsForProfile, type WorldMeta } from \"@/lib/game/worlds\";\n\n/**\n * Temporary single-world shim. It runs the legacy migration, then boots the\n * active profile's most-recent world (creating a default profile/world if\n * none exist). The profile/world selection menus replace this in a later\n * commit; for now the app still drops straight into one world.\n *\n * localStorage is read in an effect (client-only) so SSR never touches it.\n */\nexport default function HomePage() {\n  const [session, setSession] = useState<{ profile: Profile; world: WorldMeta } | null>(null);\n  const [reloadNonce, setReloadNonce] = useState(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    migrateLegacySave();\n    const profile = getActiveProfile() ?? createProfile(\"Player\", \"default\");\n    const world = worldsForProfile(profile.id)[0] ?? createWorld(profile.id, \"My World\", null);\n    setSession({ profile, world });\n  }, []);\n\n  if (!session) return <div className=\"game-root\" />;\n\n  return (\n    <MinecraftGame\n      key={`${session.world.id}:${reloadNonce}`}\n      world={session.world}\n      profile={session.profile}\n      onQuitToWorlds={() => window.location.reload()}\n      onReloadWorld={() => setReloadNonce((n) => n + 1)}\n    />\n  );\n}\n"
}

Effects are intended to synchronize state between React and external systems such as manually updating the DOM, state management libraries, or other platform APIs. In general, the body of an effect should do one or both of the following:
* Update external systems with the latest state from React.
* Subscribe for updates from some external system, calling setState in a callback function when external state changes.

> AGENT

Both are lint-rule issues. Since the component is keyed by world id, the refs only need initializing once (the world is stable for the mount). And the shim should defer its setState via `queueMicrotask`, matching the existing pattern in the hook.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01MvyazY2Dns3pav9DQwRo6b

  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "  // Stable for the mount's life (the shell keys this hook by world id), but kept\n  // in refs so the long-lived rAF/autosave effect always reads the live values.\n  const saveKeyRef = useRef(worldSaveKey(opts.world.id));\n  saveKeyRef.current = worldSaveKey(opts.world.id);\n  const worldSeedRef = useRef(opts.world.seed);\n  worldSeedRef.current = opts.world.seed;",
  "new_string": "  // The owning shell keys this hook by world id, so the world is fixed for the\n  // mount's life; capturing it once in refs lets the long-lived rAF/autosave\n  // effect read the save key and seed without re-subscribing.\n  const saveKeyRef = useRef(worldSaveKey(opts.world.id));\n  const worldSeedRef = useRef(opts.world.seed);"
}

203	  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
204	  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Robot skin" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "true");
205	  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Steve skin" })).toHaveAttribute("aria-pressed", "false");
206	});
207	
208	test("saving from the pause menu persists the world across a reload", async ({ gamePage: page }) => {
209	  await calmDaytime(page);
210	  const seed = await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.world.seed);
211	  const positionBefore = await playerPosition(page);
212	
213	  await page.keyboard.press("Escape");
214	  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Save Game" }).click();
215	  const saved = await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("minecraft_save_v7"));
216	  expect(saved).not.toBeNull();
217	  expect(JSON.parse(saved!).seed).toBe(seed);
218	  expect(JSON.parse(saved!).version).toBe(5);
219	
220	  await page.reload();
221	  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__monecraft !== undefined, undefined, { timeout: 30000 });
222	
223	  expect(await page.evaluate(() => window.__monecraft!.engine.state.world.seed)).toBe(seed);
224	  const positionAfter = await playerPosition(page);
225	  expect(Math.abs(positionAfter.x - positionBefore.x)).toBeLessThan(2);
226	  expect(Math.abs(positionAfter.z - positionBefore.z)).toBeLessThan(2);
227	});
228	
```

1	/* eslint-disable @next/next/no-img-element -- tiny generated data-URL sprites; next/image cannot optimize them */
2	
3	import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
4	import { TRANSPARENT_PIXEL } from "@/lib/ui/sprites";
5	
6	type PixelImgProps = {
7	  src: string;
8	  alt: string;
9	  size: number;
10	  className?: string;
11	  "aria-hidden"?: boolean;
12	};
13	
14	const noopSubscribe = () => () => {};
15	
16	/**
17	 * True after hydration. Sprites are canvas-generated, so the server can only
18	 * ever render the transparent fallback; the hydration render must match it
19	 * (React does not patch mismatched src attributes), and the real sprite swaps
20	 * in on the immediate follow-up render.
21	 */
22	function useHydrated(): boolean {
23	  return useSyncExternalStore(
24	    noopSubscribe,
25	    () => true,
26	    () => false
27	  );
28	}
29	
30	/** An <img> for generated pixel-art sprites: never draggable, always pixelated. */
31	export default function PixelImg({ src, alt, size, className, "aria-hidden": ariaHidden }: PixelImgProps) {
32	  const hydrated = useHydrated();
33	  return (
34	    <img
35	      src={hydrated ? src : TRANSPARENT_PIXEL}
36	      alt={alt}
37	      draggable={false}
38	      width={size}
39	      height={size}
40	      className={className}
41	      aria-hidden={ariaHidden}
42	      style={{ imageRendering: "pixelated" }}
43	    />
44	  );
45	}
46

SKIN_PRESETS, type SkinId } from \"@/lib/game/playerSkins\";\nimport { skinPortraitUrl } from \"@/lib/ui/sprites\";\n\ntype CreateProfileFormProps = {\n  onCreate: (name: string, skinId: SkinId) => void;\n  onCancel: () => void;\n};\n\n/** The new-profile form: a name and a skin picker, reusing the pause-menu swatch grid. */\nexport default function CreateProfileForm({ onCreate, onCancel }: CreateProfileFormProps) {\n  const [name, setName] = useState(\"\");\n  const [skinId, setSkinId] = useState<SkinId>(DEFAULT_SKIN_ID);\n\n  return (\n    <form\n      className=\"menu-form\"\n      onSubmit={(event) => {\n        event.preventDefault();\n        onCreate(name, skinId);\n      }}\n    >\n      <label className=\"menu-field\">\n        <span>Name</span>\n        <input\n          className=\"menu-input\"\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={name}\n          maxLength={MAX_PROFILE_NAME}\n          placeholder=\"Player\"\n          autoFocus\n          onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}\n          aria-label=\"Profile name\"\n        />\n      </label>\n      <div className=\"pause-skins\">\n        <div className=\"pause-skins-title\">Appearance</div>\n        <div className=\"pause-skins-grid\">\n          {SKIN_PRESETS.map((preset) => (\n            <button\n              key={preset.id}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"skin-swatch\"\n              aria-pressed={preset.id === skinId}\n              aria-label={`${preset.label} skin`}\n              onClick={() => setSkinId(preset.id)}\n            >\n              <PixelImg src={skinPortraitUrl(preset.id)} alt=\"\" size={32} aria-hidden />\n              <span>{preset.label}</span>\n            </button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"menu-form-actions\">\n        <button type=\"submit\" className=\"mc-button\">\n          Create\n        </button>\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"mc-button\" onClick={onCancel}>\n          Cancel\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </form>\n  );\n}\n"

}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/components/menu/ProfileSelect.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState } from \"react\";\nimport PixelImg from \"@/components/game/PixelImg\";\nimport CreateProfileForm from \"@/components/menu/CreateProfileForm\";\nimport MenuScreen from \"@/components/menu/MenuScreen\";\nimport { createProfile, deleteProfile, MAX_PROFILE_NAME, readProfiles, renameProfile } from \"@/lib/game/profiles\";\nimport { skinPortraitUrl } from \"@/lib/ui/sprites\";\nimport { deleteWorldsForProfile, worldsForProfile } from \"@/lib/game/worlds\";\n\ntype ProfileSelectProps = {\n  /** Enter a profile: select it and show its worlds. */\n  onPlay: (profileId: string) => void;\n};\n\n/** The top menu: pick a player profile, or create / rename / delete one. */\nexport default function ProfileSelect({ onPlay }: ProfileSelectProps) {\n  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [editName, setEditName] = useState(\"\");\n  const [confirmingDeleteId, setConfirmingDeleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [, force] = useState(0);\n  const refresh = () => force((n) => n + 1);\n\n  if (creating) {\n    return (\n      <MenuScreen title=\"New Profile\">\n        <CreateProfileForm\n          onCreate={(name, skinId) => {\n            const profile = createProfile(name, skinId);\n            setCreating(false);\n            onPlay(profile.id); // straight into the new profile's (empty) world list\n          }}\n          onCancel={() => setCreating(false)}\n        />\n      </MenuScreen>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { profiles } = readProfiles();\n\n  return (\n    <MenuScreen title=\"Select Profile\">\n      {profiles.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">No profiles yet. Create one to start playing.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <ul className=\"menu-list\">\n          {profiles.map((profile) => (\n            <li key={profile.id} className=\"menu-card\">\n              {editingId === profile.id ? (\n                <form\n                  className=\"menu-rename\"\n                  onSubmit={(event) => {\n                    event.preventDefault();\n                    renameProfile(profile.id, editName);\n                    setEditingId(null);\n                    refresh();\n                  }}\n                >\n                  <input\n                    className=\"menu-input\"\n                    value={editName}\n                    maxLength={MAX_PROFILE_NAME}\n                    autoFocus\n                    aria-label=\"Rename profile\"\n                    onChange={(event) => setEditName(event.target.value)}\n                  />\n                  <button type=\"submit\" className=\"mc-button\">\n                    Save\n                  </button>\n                  <button type=\"button\" className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>\n                    Cancel\n                  </button>\n                </form>\n              ) : confirmingDeleteId === profile.id ? (\n                <div className=\"menu-confirm\">\n                  <span>Delete “{profile.name}” and all its worlds?</span>\n                  <div className=\"menu-confirm-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button danger\"\n                      onClick={() => {\n                        deleteWorldsForProfile(profile.id);\n                        deleteProfile(profile.id);\n                        setConfirmingDeleteId(null);\n                        refresh();\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(null)}>\n                      Cancel\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <button className=\"menu-card-play\" data-testid={`profile-${profile.id}`} onClick={() => onPlay(profile.id)}>\n                    <PixelImg src={skinPortraitUrl(profile.skinId)} alt=\"\" size={40} aria-hidden />\n                    <span className=\"menu-card-name\">{profile.name}</span>\n                    <span className=\"menu-card-sub\">{worldsForProfile(profile.id).length} worlds</span>\n                  </button>\n                  <div className=\"menu-card-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button\"\n                      onClick={() => {\n                        setEditName(profile.name);\n                        setEditingId(profile.id);\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      Rename\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(profile.id)}>\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n      <button className=\"mc-button menu-primary\" data-testid=\"new-profile\" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>\n        New Profile\n      </button>\n    </MenuScreen>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { useState } from \"react\";\nimport CreateWorldForm from \"@/components/menu/CreateWorldForm\";\nimport MenuScreen from \"@/components/menu/MenuScreen\";\nimport type { Profile } from \"@/lib/game/profiles\";\nimport { createWorld, deleteWorld, MAX_WORLD_NAME, renameWorld, worldsForProfile } from \"@/lib/game/worlds\";\n\ntype WorldSelectProps = {\n  profile: Profile;\n  /** Enter a world (the shell records last-played and boots it). */\n  onPlay: (worldId: string) => void;\n  /** Back to the profile list. */\n  onBack: () => void;\n};\n\n/** A profile's world list: pick a world, or create / rename / delete one. */\nexport default function WorldSelect({ profile, onPlay, onBack }: WorldSelectProps) {\n  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [editName, setEditName] = useState(\"\");\n  const [confirmingDeleteId, setConfirmingDeleteId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [, force] = useState(0);\n  const refresh = () => force((n) => n + 1);\n\n  if (creating) {\n    return (\n      <MenuScreen title={`${profile.name} — New World`}>\n        <CreateWorldForm\n          onCreate={(name, seed) => {\n            const world = createWorld(profile.id, name, seed);\n            setCreating(false);\n            onPlay(world.id); // straight into the freshly created world\n          }}\n          onCancel={() => setCreating(false)}\n        />\n      </MenuScreen>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const worlds = worldsForProfile(profile.id);\n\n  return (\n    <MenuScreen title={`${profile.name} — Worlds`}>\n      {worlds.length === 0 ? (\n        <p className=\"menu-empty\">No worlds yet. Create your first world.</p>\n      ) : (\n        <ul className=\"menu-list\">\n          {worlds.map((world) => (\n            <li key={world.id} className=\"menu-card\">\n              {editingId === world.id ? (\n                <form\n                  className=\"menu-rename\"\n                  onSubmit={(event) => {\n                    event.preventDefault();\n                    renameWorld(world.id, editName);\n                    setEditingId(null);\n                    refresh();\n                  }}\n                >\n                  <input\n                    className=\"menu-input\"\n                    value={editName}\n                    maxLength={MAX_WORLD_NAME}\n                    autoFocus\n                    aria-label=\"Rename world\"\n                    onChange={(event) => setEditName(event.target.value)}\n                  />\n                  <button type=\"submit\" className=\"mc-button\">\n                    Save\n                  </button>\n                  <button type=\"button\" className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setEditingId(null)}>\n                    Cancel\n                  </button>\n                </form>\n              ) : confirmingDeleteId === world.id ? (\n                <div className=\"menu-confirm\">\n                  <span>Delete “{world.name}”? This cannot be undone.</span>\n                  <div className=\"menu-confirm-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button danger\"\n                      onClick={() => {\n                        deleteWorld(world.id);\n                        setConfirmingDeleteId(null);\n                        refresh();\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(null)}>\n                      Cancel\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n              ) : (\n                <>\n                  <button className=\"menu-card-play\" data-testid={`world-${world.id}`} onClick={() => onPlay(world.id)}>\n                    <span className=\"menu-card-name\">{world.name}</span>\n                    <span className=\"menu-card-sub\">Seed {world.seed}</span>\n                  </button>\n                  <div className=\"menu-card-actions\">\n                    <button\n                      className=\"mc-button\"\n                      onClick={() => {\n                        setEditName(world.name);\n                        setEditingId(world.id);\n                      }}\n                    >\n                      Rename\n                    </button>\n                    <button className=\"mc-button\" onClick={() => setConfirmingDeleteId(world.id)}>\n                      Delete\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </li>\n          ))}\n        </ul>\n      )}\n      <div className=\"menu-bottom-row\">\n        <button className=\"mc-button\" data-testid=\"back-to-profiles\" onClick={onBack}>\n          Back\n        </button>\n        <button className=\"mc-button menu-primary\" data-testid=\"new-world\" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>\n          New World\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </MenuScreen>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/components/GameShell.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport MinecraftGame from \"@/components/MinecraftGame\";\nimport ProfileSelect from \"@/components/menu/ProfileSelect\";\nimport WorldSelect from \"@/components/menu/WorldSelect\";\nimport { migrateLegacySave } from \"@/lib/game/legacyMigration\";\nimport { getProfile, setActiveProfile } from \"@/lib/game/profiles\";\nimport { getWorld, touchWorld } from \"@/lib/game/worlds\";\n\n/**\n * Top-level menu shell. Owns the screen state machine (profile-select ->\n * world-select -> play) and boots the legacy migration once on mount. The play\n * screen mounts MinecraftGame keyed by world id + a reload nonce, so switching\n * worlds (or Load/Reset) remounts the subtree — the game effect's cleanup\n * disposes the old engine/renderer and a fresh mount boots the next world, with\n * no page reload.\n */\ntype Screen =\n  | { name: \"profile-select\" }\n  | { name: \"world-select\"; profileId: string }\n  | { name: \"play\"; profileId: string; worldId: string };\n\nexport default function GameShell() {\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<Screen>({ name: \"profile-select\" });\n  const [reloadNonce, setReloadNonce] = useState(0);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    migrateLegacySave();\n    // Microtask hop keeps this off the synchronous effect path (cascading-render lint).\n    queueMicrotask(() => setReady(true));\n  }, []);\n\n  // localStorage is read below; hold the neutral frame until we're client-side.\n  if (!ready) return <div className=\"menu-screen\" />;\n\n  if (screen.name === \"play\") {\n    const profile = getProfile(screen.profileId);\n    const world = getWorld(screen.worldId);\n    // Both exist in normal flow; a cross-tab delete drops us back to a menu.\n    if (profile && world) {\n      return (\n        <MinecraftGame\n          key={`${world.id}:${reloadNonce}`}\n          world={world}\n          profile={profile}\n          onQuitToWorlds={() => setScreen({ name: \"world-select\", profileId: profile.id })}\n          onReloadWorld={() => setReloadNonce((nonce) => nonce + 1)}\n        />\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (screen.name === \"world-select\") {\n    const profile = getProfile(screen.profileId);\n    if (profile) {\n      return (\n        <WorldSelect\n          profile={profile}\n          onPlay={(worldId) => {\n            touchWorld(worldId);\n            setScreen({ name: \"play\", profileId: profile.id, worldId });\n          }}\n          onBack={() => setScreen({ name: \"profile-select\" })}\n        />\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ProfileSelect\n      onPlay={(profileId) => {\n        setActiveProfile(profileId);\n        setScreen({ name: \"world-select\", profileId });\n      }}\n    />\n  );\n}\n"
}

1	:root {
2	  color-scheme: light;

4	  /* Minecraft chrome palette: gray panels with light/dark bevel edges. */
5	  --mc-panel: #c6c6c6;
6	  --mc-panel-deep: #8b8b8b;
7	  --mc-bevel-light: #ffffff;
8	  --mc-bevel-dark: #373737;

13	  --mc-button: #6f6f6f;

15	  /* Item tooltip: near-black fill with Minecraft's violet gradient border. */

17	  --mc-tooltip-border-a: #28007f;
18	  --mc-tooltip-border-b: #5b00b5;
19	  --mc-tooltip-sub: #a8a8a8;
20	  /* Monospace stack, kept for the F3 debug readout (column alignment). */
21	  --mc-font-mono: "Lucida Console", Monaco, "Courier New", monospace;
22	  /* Generated noise tiles (set at runtime by installUiTiles). The chrome grain
23	     tiles default to `none` so panels fall back to their flat color pre-install
24	     and during SSR. */
25	  --mc-tile-dirt: none;
26	  --mc-tile-stone: none;
27	  --mc-tile-panel: none;
28	  --mc-tile-well: none;
29	  --mc-tile-button: none;
30	  /* Strength of the always-on screen-edge vignette (fed into rgba alpha). */
31	  --mc-vignette: 0.28;
32	}

46	  font-family: var(--mc-font), var(--mc-font-mono);

{

  "content": "/* Profile- and world-selection menus (the GameShell screens before play).\n   Reuses the .mc-button bevel and the .pause-skins swatch grid from hud.css. */\n\n.menu-screen {\n  position: fixed;\n  inset: 0;\n  z-index: 1;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  padding: 16px;\n  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #2a2d3a 0%, #15171f 100%);\n  animation: mc-fade-in 140ms ease-out;\n}\n\n.menu-panel {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 8px;\n  width: min(94vw, 420px);\n  max-height: 88vh;\n}\n\n.menu-logo {\n  text-align: center;\n  color: var(--mc-text-light);\n  font-size: 30px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  letter-spacing: 1px;\n  text-shadow: var(--mc-text-shadow);\n}\n\n.menu-title {\n  text-align: center;\n  color: var(--mc-text-light);\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  opacity: 0.85;\n  text-shadow: var(--mc-text-shadow);\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.menu-empty {\n  text-align: center;\n  color: var(--mc-text-light);\n  font-size: 13px;\n  opacity: 0.85;\n  text-shadow: var(--mc-text-shadow);\n  padding: 18px 8px;\n}\n\n.menu-list {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 6px;\n  overflow-y: auto;\n  max-height: 52vh;\n  padding-right: 2px;\n}\n\n.menu-card {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 6px;\n  align-items: stretch;\n}\n\n.menu-card-play {\n  flex: 1;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  text-align: left;\n  padding: 8px 12px;\n  font-family: inherit;\n  color: var(--mc-text-light);\n  text-shadow: var(--mc-text-shadow);\n  background: var(--mc-tile-button), var(--mc-button);\n  border-style: solid;\n  border-width: 2px;\n  border-color: #a8a8a8 #2c2c2c #2c2c2c #a8a8a8;\n  outline: 2px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.85);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  user-select: none;\n  transition: background-color 80ms ease;\n}\n\n.menu-card-play:hover,\n.menu-card-play:focus-visible {\n  background: var(--mc-button-hover);\n  border-color: #c3cbe5 #34394d #34394d #c3cbe5;\n}\n\n.menu-card-play:active {\n  border-color: #2c2c2c #a8a8a8 #a8a8a8 #2c2c2c;\n  transform: translateY(1px);\n}\n\n.menu-card-name {\n  font-size: 14px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.menu-card-sub {\n  margin-left: auto;\n  font-size: 11px;\n  opacity: 0.8;\n}\n\n.menu-card-actions {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 4px;\n}\n\n.menu-card-actions .mc-button {\n  padding: 4px 8px;\n  font-size: 11px;\n  outline-width: 1px;\n}\n\n.menu-primary {\n  width: 100%;\n}\n\n.menu-bottom-row {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 8px;\n}\n\n.menu-bottom-row .mc-button {\n  flex: 1;\n}\n\n.menu-form {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.menu-field {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 4px;\n  color:   "content": "import { describe, expect, mock, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport { render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport userEvent from \"@testing-library/user-event\";\nimport CreateWorldForm from \"@/components/menu/CreateWorldForm\";\n\ndescribe(\"CreateWorldForm\", () => {\n  test(\"submits the entered name and seed\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onCreate = mock();\n    render(<CreateWorldForm onCreate={onCreate} onCancel={mock()} />);\n\n    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"World name\"), \"Survival\");\n    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"World seed\"), \"42\");\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Create World\" }));\n\n    expect(onCreate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n    expect(onCreate).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"Survival\", \"42\");\n  });\n\n  test(\"a blank seed is allowed (random world)\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onCreate = mock();\n    render(<CreateWorldForm onCreate={onCreate} onCancel={mock()} />);\n\n    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText(\"World name\"), \"Random\");\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Create World\" }));\n\n    expect(onCreate).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"Random\", \"\");\n  });\n\n  test(\"cancel fires its callback\", async () => {\n    const user = userEvent.setup();\n    const onCancel = mock();\n    render(<CreateWorldForm onCreate={mock()} onCancel={onCancel} />);\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole(\"button\", { name: \"Cancel\" }));\n    expect(onCancel).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n});\n"
}
